\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{p}}\Big(f_{rrr},\frac{f_{rr}}{r},\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{rr},\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{p}\sim\Big|r^{\frac{m}{p}}\Big(\big(f_{r}+\frac{m}{r}f\big)_{rr},\frac{1}{r}\big(f_{r}+\frac{m}{r}f\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{p},

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{p}}\Big(f_{rrrr},\big(\frac{f_{rr}}{r}\big)_{r},\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{rrr},\Big(\frac{1}{r}(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{p}\!\sim\!\Big|r^{\frac{m}{p}}\Big(\big(f_{r}+\frac{m}{r}f\big)_{rrr},\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(f_{r}+\frac{m}{r}f\big)_{r}\Big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{p}.

Proof.

The proof can be derived via the
\boldsymbol{x}
-coordinates in
\mathbb{R}^{n}
. Let
\boldsymbol{f}(\boldsymbol{x})=f(r)\frac{\boldsymbol{x}}{r}
. First, notice that
\boldsymbol{f}
is curl-free,
i.e.

\displaystyle\mathrm{curl}\,\boldsymbol{f}=\partial_{1}f_{2}-\partial_{2}f_{1}=0

\displaystyle\text{if $n=2$},

(7.5)

\displaystyle\mathrm{curl}\,\boldsymbol{f}=(\partial_{2}f_{3}-\partial_{3}f_{2},\partial_{3}f_{1}-\partial_{1}f_{3},\partial_{1}f_{2}-\partial_{2}f_{1})^{\top}=\boldsymbol{0}

\displaystyle\text{if $n=3$},

and the divergence of
\boldsymbol{f}
takes the form:

\diver\boldsymbol{f}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\partial_{i}f_{i}=f_{r}+\frac{m}{r}f.

(7.6)

Next, combining (
7.5
)–(
7.6
) leads to the following two identities:

\displaystyle-\Delta\boldsymbol{f}=-\nabla\diver\boldsymbol{f}-\nabla^{\perp}\mathrm{curl}\,\boldsymbol{f}=-\nabla\diver\boldsymbol{f}

\displaystyle\text{if $n=2$},

\displaystyle-\Delta\boldsymbol{f}=-\nabla\diver\boldsymbol{f}+\nabla\times\mathrm{curl}\,\boldsymbol{f}=-\nabla\diver\boldsymbol{f}

\displaystyle\text{if $n=3$},

where
\nabla^{\perp}=(-\partial_{2},\partial_{1})
. This implies that

\nabla\boldsymbol{f}=-\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\diver\boldsymbol{f},

where
(-\Delta)^{-1}
is defined via the Fourier transform:

\big((-\Delta)^{-1}g\big)(\boldsymbol{x}):=\mathcal{F}^{-1}\big[\frac{1}{4\pi|\boldsymbol{\omega}|^{2}}\mathcal{F}[g](\boldsymbol{\omega})\big](\boldsymbol{x}),

with
\mathcal{F}[g](\boldsymbol{\omega})
as
the Fourier transform of
g\in C^{\infty}_{\rm c}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
,
i.e.

\mathcal{F}[g](\boldsymbol{\omega})=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}g(\boldsymbol{x})e^{-2\pi\mathrm{i}\boldsymbol{x}\cdot\boldsymbol{\omega}}\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x},

and
\mathcal{F}^{-1}[g](\boldsymbol{x})
as its inverse Fourier transform.

Thus, it follows from Lemma
A.8
that

\|\diver\boldsymbol{f}\|_{L^{p}}\leq C_{0}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{f}\|_{L^{p}}\leq C(p)\|\diver\boldsymbol{f}\|_{L^{p}},

(7.7)

which, together with (
7.6
), leads to

\|\nabla\boldsymbol{f}\|_{L^{p}}\sim\Big|r^{\frac{m}{p}}\big(f_{r}+\frac{m}{r}f\big)_{r}\Big|_{p}.

(7.8)

Finally, for the higher derivatives, one can get from (
7.7
) that

\|\nabla^{j+1}\boldsymbol{f}\|_{L^{p}}\sim\|\nabla^{j}\diver\boldsymbol{f}\|_{L^{p}}\qquad\text{for $j=1,2,3$},

(7.9)

which, along with Lemma
B.1
in Appendix
B
, gives the desired results.
∎

7.1.
Zeroth- and first-order estimates of the velocity

The first lemma concerns the zeroth-order energy estimate for
u
.

Lemma 7.2
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that, for any
t\in[0,T]
,

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u,\phi v)(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T).

Proof.

We divide the proof into two steps.

1.
Multiplying
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
by
r^{m}u
gives

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(r^{m}u^{2})_{t}+2\alpha r^{m}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)^{2}

(7.10)

\displaystyle=2\alpha\Big(\underline{r^{m}u\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)}_{:=\mathcal{B}_{7}}\Big)_{r}-r^{m}\big(uu_{r}+\phi_{r}-2\alpha\psi u_{r}\big)u.

Then we need to show that
\mathcal{B}_{7}\in W^{1,1}(I)
and
\mathcal{B}_{7}|_{r=0}=0
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
,
which allows us to apply Lemma
A.4
to obtain

\int_{0}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}_{7})_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r=-\mathcal{B}_{7}|_{r=0}=0.

(7.11)

On one hand,
\mathcal{B}_{7}|_{r=0}=0
follows from
u|_{r=0}=0
and
(u,u_{r})\in C(\bar{I})
for each
t\in(0,T]
due to (
4.4
).
On the other hand, based on (
4.3
), we see that
r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u,\frac{u}{r},u_{r},u_{rr})\in L^{2}(I)
for each
t\in(0,T]
.
Then it follow from the Hölder inequality that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{B}_{7}|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}<\infty,

\displaystyle|(\mathcal{B}_{7})_{r}|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big|\big(r^{m-1}uu_{r},r^{m}u_{r}^{2},r^{m}uu_{rr},r^{m-2}u^{2}\big)\big|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\frac{u}{r},u_{r}\big)\Big|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{rr}|_{2}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}<\infty.

Thus, integrating (
7.10
) over
I
, together with (
7.11
), yields

\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+2\alpha\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}=-\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\big(uu_{r}+\phi_{r}-2\alpha\psi u_{r}\big)u\mathrm{d}r:=\sum_{i=6}^{8}\mathcal{G}_{i}.

(7.12)

Then, for
\mathcal{G}_{6}
–
\mathcal{G}_{8}
, it follows from (
7.3
), Lemmas
4.7
,
5.3
, and
7.1
,
and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{6}

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}u^{2}u_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r\leq C_{0}|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}

(7.13)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|u|_{\infty}^{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\alpha}{8}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{7}

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi_{r}u\,\mathrm{d}r=-\frac{\gamma-1}{2\alpha}\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi(v-u)u\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi v|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi v,u)|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{8}

\displaystyle=2\alpha\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\psi uu_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r=\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}vu_{r}u\,\mathrm{d}r+\mathcal{G}_{6}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|v|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}+\mathcal{G}_{6}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2})|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\alpha}{8}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}.

Combining with (
7.12
)–(
7.13
), along with Lemma
7.1
, leads to

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+\alpha\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2})|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi v,u)|_{2}^{2}.

(7.14)

2.
For the
L^{2}(I)
-estimate of
r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi v
,
multiplying (
7.4
) by
r^{m}\phi^{2}v
and then using
\eqref{e2.2}_{1}
yield

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(r^{m}\phi^{2}v^{2})_{t}+\frac{\gamma-1}{2\alpha}r^{m}\phi^{3}v^{2}

(7.15)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}(\underline{r^{m}u\phi^{2}v^{2}}_{:=\mathcal{B}_{8}})_{r}+\big(\frac{3}{2}-\gamma\big)r^{m}\phi^{2}v^{2}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)+\frac{\gamma-1}{2\alpha}r^{m}\phi^{3}vu.

Next, we need to show that
\mathcal{B}_{8}\in W^{1,1}(I)
and
\mathcal{B}_{8}|_{r=0}=0
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
, which allows us to apply Lemma
A.4
to obtain

\int_{0}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}_{8})_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r=-\mathcal{B}_{8}|_{r=0}=0.

(7.16)

On one hand,
\mathcal{B}_{8}|_{r=0}=0
follows from (
6.9
), and

u|_{r=0}=0
and
(\phi,u)\in C(\bar{I})
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T]
due to (
4.3
). On the other hand, (
4.3
)–(
4.4
) imply that

\Big(\phi,u,\frac{u}{r},u_{r},\psi\Big)\in L^{\infty}(I),\quad r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{r},u,u_{r},\psi_{r})\in L^{2}(I)\qquad\mbox{for {\it a.e.} $t\in(0,T)$},

Then it follows from (
7.3
)
and the Hölder inequality that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{B}_{8}|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|u|_{\infty}\big(|\phi|_{\infty}^{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}^{2}\big)<\infty,

\displaystyle|(\mathcal{B}_{8})_{r}|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big|\big(r^{m-1}u\phi^{2}v^{2},r^{m}u_{r}\phi^{2}v^{2},r^{m}u\phi\phi_{r}v^{2},r^{m}u\phi^{2}vv_{r}\big)\big|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|\big(\frac{u}{r},u_{r}\big)\Big|_{\infty}\big(|\phi|_{\infty}^{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}^{2}\big)+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}|(u,\psi)|_{\infty}^{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{2}|(u,\psi)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{r},\psi_{r})|_{2}<\infty.

Thus, integrating (
7.15
) over
I
, we obtain from (
7.16
), Lemmas
4.7
, and
5.3
,
and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi v|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\gamma-1}{2\alpha}\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\frac{3}{2}}v\big|_{2}^{2}

(7.17)

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{3}{2}-\gamma\Big)\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi^{2}v^{2}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\,\mathrm{d}r+\frac{\gamma-1}{2\alpha}\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi^{3}vu\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}|v|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi v|_{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi v|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi v,u)|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\alpha}{8}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2},

which, together with (
7.14
), gives

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi v,u)|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\alpha}{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2})|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi v,u)|_{2}^{2}.

(7.18)

Applying the Grönwall inequality to the above inequality, together with Lemma
6.1
, yields that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi v,u)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{0}v_{0},u_{0})|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T).

(7.19)

Here, it remains to check the
L^{2}(I)
-boundedness of
r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{0}v_{0},u_{0})
. Indeed, it follows from Lemmas
A.1
,
A.11
, and
B.1
that

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{0}v_{0},u_{0})|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{0}u_{0},(\phi_{0})_{r},u_{0})|_{2}\leq C_{0}\big(\|\phi_{0}\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\phi_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\big)

(7.20)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(\|\phi_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\phi_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\big)\leq C_{0}.

The proof of Lemma
7.2
is completed.
∎

The following lemma concerns the first-order energy estimate of
u
.

Lemma 7.3
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that, for any
t\in[0,T]
,

\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)(t)\Big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T).

Proof.

First, multiplying
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
by
r^{m}u_{t}
gives

\displaystyle r^{m}u_{t}^{2}+\alpha\Big(r^{m}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)^{2}\Big)_{t}

(7.21)

\displaystyle=2\alpha\Big(\underline{r^{m}u_{t}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)}_{:=\mathcal{B}_{9}}\Big)_{r}-r^{m}\big(uu_{r}+\phi_{r}-2\alpha\psi u_{r}\big)u_{t}.

Next, we need to show that
\mathcal{B}_{9}\in W^{1,1}(I)
and
\mathcal{B}_{9}|_{r=0}=0
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
, which allows us to apply Lemma
A.4
to obtain

\int_{0}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}_{9})_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r=-\mathcal{B}_{9}|_{r=0}=0.

(7.22)

On one hand, by (
4.3
),
r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{t},\frac{u_{t}}{r},u_{tr})\in L^{2}([0,T];L^{2}(I))
so that
r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}\in L^{2}([0,T];H^{1}(I))
, which implies from Lemma
A.1

and
u|_{r=0}=0
that

r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}\in L^{2}([0,T];C(\bar{I}))\implies r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{r=0}=0\qquad\text{ for {\it a.e.} $t\in(0,T)$}.

(7.23)

This, together with
(u_{r},\frac{u}{r})\in C(\bar{I})
for
t\in(0,T]
due to (
4.4
), yields that
\mathcal{B}_{9}|_{r=0}=0
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
. On the other hand, to obtain
\mathcal{B}_{9}\in W^{1,1}(I)
, by (
4.3
), we have

r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\frac{u}{r},u_{r},u_{t},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r},u_{rr},\frac{u_{t}}{r},u_{tr}\Big)\in L^{2}(I)\qquad\text{for {\it a.e.} $t\in(0,T)$}.

This implies from the Hölder inequality that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{B}_{9}|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}<\infty,

\displaystyle|(\mathcal{B}_{9})_{r}|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|r^{m-1}u_{t}\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{1}+C_{0}\Big|r^{m}u_{tr}\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{1}+C_{0}\Big|r^{m}u_{t}\Big(u_{rr},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{rr},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}<\infty.

Therefore, integrating (
7.21
) over
I
, together with (
7.22
), yields

\alpha\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}=-\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\big(uu_{r}+\phi_{r}-2\alpha\psi u_{r}\big)u_{t}\,\mathrm{d}r:=\sum_{i=9}^{11}\mathcal{G}_{i}.

(7.24)

Then, for
\mathcal{G}_{9}
–
\mathcal{G}_{11}
, it follows from
(
7.3
), Lemmas
4.7
,
5.3
,
and
7.2
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{9}

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}uu_{r}u_{t}\,\mathrm{d}r\leq C_{0}|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}\leq C_{0}|u|_{\infty}^{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{8}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2},

(7.25)

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{10}

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi_{r}u_{t}\,\mathrm{d}r=-\frac{\gamma-1}{2\alpha}\int r^{m}\phi(v-u)u_{t}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi v|_{2}+|\phi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}\leq C(T)+\frac{1}{8}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|^{2}_{2},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{11}

\displaystyle=2\alpha\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\psi u_{r}u_{t}\,\mathrm{d}r=\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}vu_{r}u_{t}\,\mathrm{d}r+\mathcal{G}_{9}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|v|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+\mathcal{G}_{9}\leq C(T)(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2})|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{8}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}.

Substituting (
7.25
) into (
7.24
), we obtain
from Lemma
7.1
that

\displaystyle\alpha\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2})|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}^{2}+C(T)

(7.26)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2})\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}+C(T),

which, along with Lemmas
6.1
and
7.1
, and the Grönwall inequality, yields that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big((u_{0})_{r},\frac{u_{0}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+C(T)\leq C(T).

(7.27)

This completes the proof.
∎

7.2.
The second-order estimates of the velocity

Lemma 7.4
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\mbox{for any $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

We divide the proof into two steps.

1.
It follows from Lemmas
7.1
–
7.2
,
A.2
, and
B.1
that

\displaystyle|u|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\|\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C_{0}\|\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{4-n}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{n}{4}}

(7.28)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}^{\frac{4-n}{4}}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{\frac{n}{4}}\leq C(T)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{\frac{n}{4}}.

Then, multiplying
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
by
r^{\frac{m}{2}}
and taking the
L^{2}(I)
-norm of the resulting equality, we obtain from
from (
7.28
), Lemmas
4.7
,
5.3
,
and
7.2
–
7.3
, and the Young inequality that

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}uu_{r}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi u_{r}|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}uu_{r}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi(v-u)|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(v-u)u_{r}|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}|(v,u)|_{\infty}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi v|_{2}+|\phi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\bigg(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{\frac{n}{4}}+1\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+1\big)+\frac{1}{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{2},

which, along with (
7.28
) and Lemma
7.1
, leads to

|u|_{\infty}+\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{rr},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+1\big).

(7.29)

Next, it follows from (
7.29
), Lemmas
6.1
,
7.3
,
A.1
, and
A.3
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{\infty}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{\infty}

(7.30)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{m+1}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{m+1}{2}}u_{rr}|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}(r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r})_{r}|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|\chi^{\flat}r^{\frac{1}{2}}|_{\infty}+|\chi^{\sharp}r^{-1}|_{\infty}+1\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}+C_{0}(|\chi^{\flat}r^{\frac{1}{2}}|_{\infty}+1)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{rr}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{rr}|_{2}+1\big)\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+1\big).

Finally, according to (
7.3
), Lemmas
4.7
,
5.3
, and
7.2
, we have

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi(v-u)|_{2}\leq C_{0}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi v|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}|\phi|_{\infty}\big)\leq C(T).

(7.31)

Multiplying
\eqref{e2.2}_{1}
by
r^{\frac{m}{2}}
and taking the
L^{2}(I)
-norm of the resulting equation, we obtain from (
7.29
),
(
7.31
), and Lemmas
4.7
and
7.3
that

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u\phi_{r}|_{2}+(\gamma-1)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}

(7.32)

\displaystyle\leq|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{rr},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+1\big).

2.

Applying
r^{m}u_{t}\partial_{t}
to both sides of
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
, together with
\eqref{e2.2}_{3}
, gives

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(r^{m}u_{t}^{2})_{t}+2\alpha r^{m}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)^{2}

(7.33)

\displaystyle=\Big(2\alpha r^{m}u_{t}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)-r^{m}u_{t}\phi_{t}\Big)_{r}-r^{m}u_{t}(uu_{r})_{t}+r^{m}\phi_{t}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)

\displaystyle+2\alpha r^{m}\psi u_{t}u_{tr}+2\alpha r^{m}\psi_{t}u_{t}u_{r}

\displaystyle=\Big(2\alpha r^{m}u_{t}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)-r^{m}u_{t}\phi_{t}\Big)_{r}-r^{m}u_{t}(uu_{r})_{t}+r^{m}\phi_{t}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)

\displaystyle+2\alpha r^{m}\psi u_{t}u_{tr}-2\alpha r^{m}(\psi u)_{r}u_{t}u_{r}-2\alpha r^{m}\big(u_{t}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}u_{t}u_{r}

\displaystyle=\Big(\underline{2\alpha r^{m}u_{t}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)-r^{m}u_{t}\phi_{t}-2\alpha r^{m}\psi uu_{t}u_{r}}_{:=\mathcal{B}_{10}}\Big)_{r}-r^{m}u_{t}(uu_{r})_{t}

\displaystyle+r^{m}\phi_{t}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)+2\alpha r^{m}\psi u_{t}u_{tr}

\displaystyle+2\alpha r^{m}\psi u\big(u_{tr}u_{r}+u_{t}u_{rr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}u_{r}\big)-2\alpha r^{m}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}u_{t}u_{r}.

Next, we need to show that
\mathcal{B}_{10}\in W^{1,1}(I)
and
\mathcal{B}_{10}|_{r=0}=0
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
, which allows us to apply Lemma
A.4
to obtain

\int_{0}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}_{10})_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r=-\mathcal{B}_{10}|_{r=0}=0.

(7.34)

We first show that
\mathcal{B}_{10}|_{r=0}=0
. Indeed, according to (
6.9
), (
7.23
), and
(u,u_{r})\in C(\bar{I})
for
t\in(0,T]
due to (
4.4
), it remains to show that

r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{r=0}<\infty,\quad\ r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{t}|_{r=0}<\infty\qquad\text{for {\it a.e.} $t\in(0,T)$}.

(7.35)

Thanks to (
4.3
), we see that
r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r},u_{trr},(\frac{u_{t}}{r})_{r}\big)\in L^{2}(I)
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
, which, along with Lemma
7.1
, implies that

r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t},(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t})_{r}\big)\in L^{2}(I)\qquad\,\,\ \text{for \ {\it a.e.} $t\in(0,T)$}.

(7.36)

If
n=2
(
m=1
), (
7.36
) implies that

r\big(u_{tr}+\frac{1}{r}u_{t},(u_{tr}+\frac{1}{r}u_{t})_{r}\big)\in L^{2}(0,1)
.
Thus, it follows from Lemma
A.3
that

r^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{1}{r}u_{t}\big)=r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\in C([0,1])\,\qquad\text{for \ {\it a.e.} $t\in(0,T)$}.

If
n=3
(
m=2
), we deduce from Lemmas
A.1
and
A.3
that, for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
,

r\big(u_{tr}+\frac{2}{r}u_{t}\big)\in H^{1}(0,1)\implies r\big(u_{tr}+\frac{2}{r}u_{t}\big)\in C([0,1]),

that is,
r(u_{tr}+\frac{2}{r}u_{t})|_{r=0}<\infty
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
.
Similarly, the method mentioned above can be applied to show that

r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{t}|_{r=0}<\infty
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
, since
r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{t},\phi_{tr})\in L^{2}(I)
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
due to (
4.3
).

Next, we show that
\mathcal{B}_{10}\in W^{1,1}(I)
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
. By (
4.3
)–(
4.4
), we have

(\psi,u,u_{r})\in L^{\infty}(I),\,\,\,\,r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\phi_{t},u_{r},u_{t},u_{rr},\frac{u_{t}}{r},u_{tr},\big(\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)_{r},u_{trr},\psi_{r}\Big)\in L^{2}(I)\qquad\mbox{for {\it a.e.} $t\in(0,T)$}.

Then it follows from the Hölder inequality that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{B}_{10}|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{t}|_{2}+|\psi|_{\infty}|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}\Big)<\infty,

(7.37)

\displaystyle|(\mathcal{B}_{10})_{r}|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|r^{m-1}u_{t}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r},\phi_{t},\psi uu_{r}\big)\Big|_{1}+C_{0}\Big|r^{m}u_{tr}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r},\phi_{t},\psi uu_{r}\big)\Big|_{1}

\displaystyle+C_{0}\Big|r^{m}u_{t}\Big(u_{trr},\big(\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)_{r},\phi_{tr},\psi_{r}uu_{r},\psi(u_{r})^{2},\psi uu_{rr}\Big)\Big|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r},\phi_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}+|\psi|_{\infty}|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{trr},\big(\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)_{r},\phi_{tr}\Big)\Big|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{r}|_{2}|u|_{\infty}|u_{r}|_{\infty}\Big)

\displaystyle+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}\big(|\psi|_{\infty}|u_{r}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}+|\psi|_{\infty}|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{rr}|_{2}\big)<\infty,

which implies the assertion.

Now, integrating (
7.33
) over
I
, together with (
7.34
), yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|^{2}_{2}+2\alpha\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

(7.38)

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}(uu_{r})_{t}u_{t}\,\mathrm{d}r+\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi_{t}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+\!2\alpha\!\int_{0}^{\infty}\!r^{m}\Big(\psi\big(u_{t}u_{tr}+u(u_{tr}u_{r}+u_{t}u_{rr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}u_{r})\big)\!-\!\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}u_{t}u_{r}\!\Big)\mathrm{d}r\!:=\!\sum_{i=13}^{15}\mathcal{G}_{i}.

For
\mathcal{G}_{13}
–
\mathcal{G}_{14}
, it follows from (
7.29
)–(
7.30
), (
7.32
), Lemma
7.1
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{13}

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}(uu_{tr}+u_{r}u_{t})u_{t}\,\mathrm{d}r

(7.39)

\displaystyle\leq|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tr}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+\big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}u_{t}|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}u_{t}|_{2}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tr}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{2-m}{2}}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-\frac{m}{2}}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|(u,r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r})|_{\infty}^{2}+1\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\alpha}{8}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{4}+1\big)+\frac{\alpha}{8}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{14}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi_{t}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\,\mathrm{d}r\leq|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{t}|_{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+1\big)+\frac{\alpha}{8}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{4}+1\big)+\frac{\alpha}{8}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}.

For
\mathcal{G}_{15}
, it follows from (
7.3
), (
7.29
), Lemmas
7.1
and
7.3
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{15}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}(v-u)u_{t}u_{tr}\,\mathrm{d}r+\int_{0}^{\infty}(v-u)u\big(u_{tr}u_{r}+u_{t}u_{rr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}u_{r}\big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\quad-2\alpha\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}u_{t}u_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq|(v,u)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tr}|_{2}+|(v,u)|_{\infty}|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{rr}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+|(v,u)|_{\infty}|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{rr},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}\big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}u_{t}|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}u_{t}|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq|(v,u)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tr}|_{2}+|(v,u)|_{\infty}|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{rr}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}

(7.40)

\displaystyle\quad+|(v,u)|_{\infty}|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{rr},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{\infty}\big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{2-m}{2}}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-\frac{m}{2}}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+1\big)\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+1\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{4}+1\big)+\frac{\alpha}{8}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}.

Substituting (
7.39
)–(
7.40
) into (
7.38
), along with Lemma
7.1
, gives

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|^{2}_{2}+\alpha\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{4}+1\big).

Integrating above over
(\tau,t)(\tau\in(0,t))
yields

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}(t)|_{2}^{2}+\alpha\int_{\tau}^{t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}(\tau)|_{2}^{2}+C(T)\int_{\tau}^{t}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{4}+1\big)\,\mathrm{d}s.

(7.41)

For the
L^{2}(I)
-boundedness of
r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}(\tau)

on the right-hand side of the above, we multiply
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
by
r^{\frac{m}{2}}
and then take the
L^{2}(I)
-norm of the resulting equality to obtain

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}(\tau)|_{2}\leq C_{0}\Big(|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}+\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}+|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}\Big)(\tau),

which, along with the time-continuity of
(\phi,u,\psi)
and
Lemmas
7.1
,
A.1
, and
B.1
, yields

\displaystyle\limsup_{\tau\to 0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}(\tau)|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big(|u_{0}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{0})_{r}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{0})_{r}|_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+C_{0}\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big((u_{0})_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u_{0}\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}+|\psi_{0}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{0})_{r}|_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\phi_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+C_{0}\big(\|\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\boldsymbol{\psi}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{H^{2}}+\|\boldsymbol{\psi}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}+1\big)\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{H^{2}}+C_{0}\|\nabla\phi_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C_{0}.

Consequently, based on the above discussions, letting
\tau\to 0
in (
7.41
), then it follows from Lemma
7.3
and the Grönwall inequality that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T).

(7.42)

This completes the proof of Lemma
7.4
.
∎

With the help of (
7.29
) and Lemma
7.4
, we can also obtain the following estimates:

Lemma 7.5
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that, for any
t\in[0,T]
,

|(u,r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r})(t)|_{\infty}+\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\phi_{r},\phi_{t},u_{rr},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)(t)\Big|_{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\Big|\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{\infty}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T).

Proof.

First, it follows from (
7.29
)–(
7.32
) and Lemma
7.4
that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

|(u,r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r})(t)|_{\infty}+\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\phi_{r},\phi_{t},u_{rr},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)(t)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}(t)|_{2}+1\big)\leq C(T).

(7.43)

Next, multiplying
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
by
r^{\frac{m}{4}}
and then taking the
L^{4}(I)
-norm of the resulting equality, we obtain from (
7.3
), (
7.43
), and Lemmas
7.1
,
A.2
, and
B.1
that

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{4}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{4}\leq C_{0}\big|r^{\frac{m}{4}}\big(u_{t},uu_{r},\phi_{r},\psi u_{r}\big)\big|_{4}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{4}}u_{t}|_{4}+|(u,v)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{4}}u_{r}|_{4}+|r^{\frac{m}{4}}\phi(v-u)|_{4}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)|r^{\frac{m}{4}}(u_{t},u_{r})|_{4}+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|(u,v)|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi v|_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\phi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\|(\boldsymbol{u}_{t},\nabla\boldsymbol{u})\|_{L^{4}}+1\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\|\boldsymbol{u}_{t}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{4-n}{4}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{u}_{t}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{n}{4}}+\|\nabla\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{4-n}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{n}{4}}+1\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{\frac{4-n}{4}}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}^{\frac{n}{4}}+\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}^{\frac{4-n}{4}}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{rr},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}^{\frac{n}{4}}+1\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}^{\frac{n}{4}}+1\Big),

which, together with Lemmas
A.2
and
B.1
, and the Young inequality, yields that

\displaystyle\Big|\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C_{0}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{4-n}{n+4}}\|\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{2n}{n+4}}

(7.44)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}^{\frac{4-n}{n+4}}\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}^{\frac{n^{2}}{2(n+4)}}+1\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}^{\frac{n^{2}}{2(n+4)}}+1\Big)\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}+1\Big).

Thus, taking the square of the above and integrating the resulting inequality, along with Lemma
7.4
, lead to the desired conclusion.
∎

We now show the estimate of
\psi
.

Lemma 7.6
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

|\psi(t)|_{\infty}+\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\psi_{r},\frac{\psi}{r},\psi_{t}\big)(t)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T)\qquad\,\mbox{for any $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

First, it follows from (
7.3
)
and Lemmas
5.3
and
7.5
that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

|\psi(t)|_{\infty}\leq C_{0}|(v,u)(t)|_{\infty}\leq C(T).

Next, the equation of
\boldsymbol{v}
in the
M-D coordinates (see (
11.64
) in §
11
) takes the form:

\boldsymbol{v}_{t}+\boldsymbol{u}\cdot\nabla\boldsymbol{v}+\frac{\gamma-1}{2\alpha}\phi(\boldsymbol{v}-\boldsymbol{u})=\boldsymbol{0}.

(7.45)

Applying the divergence to both sides of the above leads to
the following equation holds in the sense of distributions:

\displaystyle(\diver\boldsymbol{v})_{t}+\diver\big(\boldsymbol{u}(\diver\boldsymbol{v})\big)

\displaystyle=(\diver\boldsymbol{u})(\diver\boldsymbol{v})-\nabla\boldsymbol{u}^{\top}:\nabla\boldsymbol{v}

(7.46)

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma-1}{2\alpha}\nabla\phi\cdot(\boldsymbol{v}-\boldsymbol{u})-\frac{\gamma-1}{2\alpha}\phi(\diver\boldsymbol{v}-\diver\boldsymbol{u}):=\tilde{\mathcal{G}}.

Using
\boldsymbol{v}=\boldsymbol{u}+2\alpha\nabla\log\rho

and Lemmas
3.3
and
A.1
,
it can be checked that

\diver\boldsymbol{v}\in L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{n})),\qquad\boldsymbol{u}\in L^{1}([0,T];W^{1,\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{n})),

(7.47)

and
\tilde{\mathcal{G}}\in L^{1}([0,T];L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{n}))
.
Thus, it follows from Lemma
A.12
that, for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
,

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\diver\boldsymbol{v}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\gamma-1}{\alpha}\big\|\phi^{\frac{1}{2}}\diver\boldsymbol{v}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}(\diver\boldsymbol{u})(\diver\boldsymbol{v})^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}-2\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}(\nabla\boldsymbol{u}^{\top}:\nabla\boldsymbol{v})(\diver\boldsymbol{v})\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\gamma-1}{\alpha}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\big(-\nabla\phi\cdot(\boldsymbol{v}-\boldsymbol{u})+\phi(\diver\boldsymbol{u})\big)(\diver\boldsymbol{v})\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x},

which, along with the spherical coordinate transformation, yields that,
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
,

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\gamma-1}{\alpha}\Big|(r^{m}\phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

(7.48)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r-2\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\Big(u_{r}v_{r}+\frac{m}{r^{2}}uv\Big)\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+\frac{\gamma-1}{\alpha}\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\Big(-\phi_{r}(v-u)+\phi\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big)\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)\,\mathrm{d}r.

We now continue to estimate the right-hand side of (
7.48
).
It follows from Lemmas
4.7
,
7.1
,
7.3
, and
7.5
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\gamma-1}{\alpha}\Big|(r^{m}\phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

(7.49)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{\infty}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+C_{0}\Big|\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{\infty}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r},\frac{v}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}\Big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}|(v,u)|_{\infty}+|\phi|_{\infty}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}\Big)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{\infty}^{2}+1\Big)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+C(T),

which, along with Lemmas
7.1
,
7.5
, and
B.1
, and the Grönwall inequality, gives

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r},\frac{v}{r}\big)(t)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big((v_{0})_{r},\frac{v_{0}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}+C(T)

(7.50)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big((u_{0})_{r},\frac{u_{0}}{r},(\log\rho_{0})_{rr},\frac{(\log\rho_{0})_{r}}{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}+C(T)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\|(\nabla\boldsymbol{u}_{0},\nabla^{2}\log\rho_{0})\|_{L^{2}}+C(T)\leq C(T).

This, together with (
7.3
) and Lemma
7.3
, yields that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\psi_{r},\frac{\psi}{r}\big)(t)\Big|_{2}\leq C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r},\frac{v}{r}\big)(t)\Big|_{2}+C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)(t)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T).

(7.51)

Finally, multiplying
\eqref{e2.2}_{3}
by
r^{\frac{m}{2}}
and then taking the
L^{2}(I)
-norm of the resulting equality, we obtain from the above and Lemmas
7.3
and
7.5
that

\begin{split}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{t}|_{2}&\leq|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\psi u)_{r}|_{2}+\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}\\
&\leq|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}|\psi|_{\infty}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{r}|_{2}|u|_{\infty}+\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{rr},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T).\end{split}

(7.52)

The proof of Lemma
7.6
is completed.
∎

The following lemma provides the high-order estimates of
(\phi,u)
.

Lemma 7.7
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that, for any
t\in[0,T]
,

\begin{split}&\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\phi_{rr},\frac{\phi_{r}}{r},\phi_{tr}\Big)(t)\Big|^{2}_{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{rrr},\frac{u_{rr}}{r},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{rr},\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T).\end{split}

Proof.

We divide the proof into two steps.

1. Estimates on
\phi
.
For the
L^{2}(I)
-estimates of
r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{rr},\frac{\phi_{r}}{r})
and
r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{tr}
, it follows from Lemmas
4.7
and
7.5
–
7.6
that

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\phi_{rr},\frac{\phi_{r}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle=(\gamma-1)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big((\phi\psi)_{r},\frac{\phi\psi}{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}\leq(\gamma-1)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\phi_{r}\psi,\phi\psi_{r},\frac{\phi\psi}{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}

(7.53)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\psi_{r},\frac{\psi}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T),

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{tr}|_{2}

\displaystyle=(\gamma-1)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi\psi)_{t}|_{2}\leq(\gamma-1)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{t}\psi,\phi\psi_{t})|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{t}|_{2}+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{t}|_{2}\leq C(T).

2. Estimates on
u
.
First, multiplying
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
by
r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}
and taking
the
L^{2}(I)
-norm of the resulting equality, we obtain from Lemmas
4.7
,
7.3
, and
7.6
that

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+C_{0}|u_{r}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}(u,\psi)|_{2}+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\psi|_{2}

(7.54)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+|u_{r}|_{\infty}+1\big).

Similarly, applying
r^{\frac{m}{2}}\partial_{r}
on both sides of
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
and taking
L^{2}(I)
-norm of the resulting equality,
we obtain from Lemmas
4.7
,
7.3
,
and
7.5
–
7.6
that

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{rr}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},(uu_{r})_{r},\phi_{rr},2\alpha(\psi u_{r})_{r}\big)\big|_{2}

(7.55)

\displaystyle\leq|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tr}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}^{2}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}uu_{rr}|_{2}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}\psi|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi\psi_{r}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{r}u_{r}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi u_{rr}|_{2})

\displaystyle\leq|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tr}|_{2}+|u_{r}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}+C_{0}|(u,\psi)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{rr}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}\big(|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}+|(\phi,u_{r})|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{r}|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tr}|_{2}+|u_{r}|_{\infty}+1\big).

Thus, combining with (
7.54
)–(
7.55
), together with Lemmas
7.1
and
7.4
–
7.5
, gives

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{rrr},\frac{u_{rr}}{r},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{rr},\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\int_{0}^{t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{rr},\frac{1}{r}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+|u_{r}|_{\infty}^{2}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}s+C(T)\leq C(T).

This completes the proof of Lemma
7.7
.
∎

7.3.
Time-weighted energy estimates of the velocity

Lemma 7.8
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

\begin{split}&t\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)(t)\Big|^{2}_{2}+\int_{0}^{t}s|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|^{2}_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\mbox{for any $t\in[0,T]$}.\end{split}

Proof.

First, applying
r^{m}u_{tt}\partial_{t}
to both sides of
(\ref {e2.2})_{2}
and integrating the resulting equality over
I
yield that, for any
t\in[\tau,T]
and
\tau\in(0,T)
,

\displaystyle\underline{-2\alpha\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{tr}u_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}r}_{:=\mathcal{B}_{11}}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}

(7.56)

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\big((uu_{r})_{t}+\phi_{tr}-2\alpha(\psi u_{r})_{t}\big)u_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}r.

Then we claim that, for any
t\in[\tau,T]
,

\mathcal{B}_{11}=\alpha\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}.

(7.57)

From the spherical coordinate transformation
and Lemma
B.1
, it suffices to prove that

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\diver\boldsymbol{f}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=-2\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\nabla\diver\boldsymbol{f}\cdot\boldsymbol{f}_{t}\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}

(7.58)

for any vector function
\boldsymbol{f}\in L^{2}([\tau,T];H^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{n}))
with

\boldsymbol{f}_{t}\in L^{2}([\tau,T];L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{n}))
.
To achieve this, consider the standard mollifier

\omega_{\varepsilon}
defined on
\mathbb{R}
, and set

g^{\varepsilon}(t,\boldsymbol{x})=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}g(t-t^{\prime},\boldsymbol{x})\omega_{\varepsilon}(t^{\prime})\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}.

Then, after extension and regularization,
\boldsymbol{f}^{\varepsilon}\in C^{\infty}([\tau,T];H^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{n}))
, we see from integration by parts that, for any
\varepsilon,\iota>0
,

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\diver\boldsymbol{f}^{\varepsilon}-\diver\boldsymbol{f}^{\iota}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=2\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\big(\diver\boldsymbol{f}^{\varepsilon}-\diver\boldsymbol{f}^{\iota}\big)\big(\diver(\boldsymbol{f}_{t})^{\varepsilon}-\diver(\boldsymbol{f}_{t})^{\iota}\big)\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}

\displaystyle=-2\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\big(\nabla\diver\boldsymbol{f}^{\varepsilon}-\nabla\diver\boldsymbol{f}^{\iota}\big)\cdot\big((\boldsymbol{f}_{t})^{\varepsilon}-(\boldsymbol{f}_{t})^{\iota}\big)\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}.

Integrating above over
[\tau,T]
, along with the Young inequality, yields

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[\tau,T]}\|\diver\boldsymbol{f}^{\varepsilon}-\diver\boldsymbol{f}^{\iota}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\diver\boldsymbol{f}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)-\diver\boldsymbol{f}^{\iota}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{T}\big(\|\nabla\diver(\boldsymbol{f}^{\varepsilon}-\boldsymbol{f}^{\iota})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\boldsymbol{f}_{t})^{\varepsilon}-(\boldsymbol{f}_{t})^{\iota}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\big)\,\mathrm{d}t.

Letting
(\varepsilon,\iota)\to(0,0)
in the above inequality
implies that
\{\diver\boldsymbol{f}^{\varepsilon}\}_{\varepsilon>0}
is a Cauchy sequence in
C([\tau,T];L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{n}))
, and hence
\diver\boldsymbol{f}^{\varepsilon}
converges to
\diver\boldsymbol{f}\in C([\tau,T];L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{n}))
in
C([\tau,T];L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{n}))
as
\varepsilon\to 0
. Similarly, we obtain from the above calculation that,
for all
s,t\in[\tau,T]
,

\|\diver\boldsymbol{f}^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=\|\diver\boldsymbol{f}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-2\int_{s}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\nabla\diver\boldsymbol{f}^{\varepsilon}\cdot(\boldsymbol{f}_{t})^{\varepsilon}\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}\,\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}.

Passing to the limit
\varepsilon\to 0
leads to

\|\diver\boldsymbol{f}(t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=\|\diver\boldsymbol{f}(s)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-2\int_{s}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\nabla\diver\boldsymbol{f}\cdot\boldsymbol{f}_{t}\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}\,\mathrm{d}s^{\prime},

which implies that the mapping:
t\to\|\diver\boldsymbol{f}(t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}
is absolutely continuous. Thus, differentiating above with respect to
t

leads to (
7.58
) so that claim (
7.57
) holds.

Now, substituting (
7.57
) into (
7.56
), we obtain
from (
7.44
), Lemmas
7.1
and
7.4
–
7.7
,
and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

\displaystyle\alpha\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}=-\int r^{m}\big((uu_{r})_{t}+\phi_{tr}-2\alpha(\psi u_{r})_{t}\big)u_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}r

(7.59)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{t},\psi_{t})|_{2}|u_{r}|_{\infty}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tr}|_{2}|(u,\psi)|_{\infty}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{tr}|_{2}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}+1\Big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+1\Big)+\frac{1}{8}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}.

Multiplying above by
t
and integrating the resulting inequality over
[\tau,t]
, along with Lemmas
7.1
and
7.4
, imply that

t\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)(t)\Big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{t}s|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\Big(\tau\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)(\tau)\Big|_{2}^{2}+1\Big).

(7.60)

Next, thanks to Lemma
7.4
,
r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r})\in L^{2}([0,T];L^{2}(I))
. It follows from
Lemma
A.6
that there exists a sequence
\{\tau_{k}\}_{k=1}^{\infty}
such that

\tau_{k}\to 0,\quad\tau_{k}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)(\tau_{k})\Big|_{2}^{2}\to 0\qquad\,\,\text{as $k\to\infty$}.

(7.61)

Choosing
\tau=\tau_{k}\to 0
in (
7.60
) yields that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

t\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)(t)\Big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}s|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T).

(7.62)

The proof of Lemma
7.8
is completed.
∎

Lemma 7.9
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that, for any
t\in[0,T]
,

t\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{rrr},\frac{u_{rr}}{r},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{rr},\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)(t)\Big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}s\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{trr},\big(\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T).

Proof.

First, it follows from (
7.44
) by multiplying
\sqrt{t}
and Lemma
7.8
that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

\sqrt{t}\Big|\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{\infty}\leq C(T)\Big(\sqrt{t}\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}+1\Big)\leq C(T),

(7.63)

which, along with (
7.54
)–(
7.55
), (
7.63
), and Lemmas
7.1
and
7.8
, leads to

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{rrr},\frac{u_{rr}}{r},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{rr},\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}

(7.64)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\sqrt{t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{rr},\frac{1}{r}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\sqrt{t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}+\sqrt{t}|u_{r}|_{\infty}+1\Big)\leq C(T).

Next, applying
\sqrt{t}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\partial_{t}
to
(\ref {e2.2})_{2}
and taking
L^{2}(I)
-norm of the resulting equality, along with (
7.63
), and Lemmas
7.1
and
7.4
–
7.8
, we have

\begin{split}&\sqrt{t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{trr},\big(\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}\leq C_{0}\sqrt{t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{tr}\Big|_{2}\\
&\leq C_{0}\sqrt{t}\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tt},(uu_{r})_{t},\phi_{tr},(\psi u_{r})_{t}\big)\big|_{2}\\
&\leq C_{0}\sqrt{t}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{t},\psi_{t})|_{2}|u_{r}|_{\infty}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tr}|_{2}|(u,\psi)|_{\infty}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{tr}|_{2}\big)\\
&\leq C(T)\big(\sqrt{t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}+1\big).\end{split}

(7.65)

This, along with Lemma
7.8
, implies that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

\int_{0}^{t}s\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{trr},\big(\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\int_{0}^{t}s|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s+C(T)\leq C(T).

(7.66)

This completes the proof of Lemma
7.9
.
∎

8.
Global Estimates for the
3
-Order Regular Solutions with Far-Field Vacuum

This section is devoted to establishing the global estimates for the
3
-order regular solutions when
\bar{\rho}=0
.
Let
T>0
be any fixed time,
and let
(\rho,u)(t,r)
be the
3
-order regular solution of
problem (
4.2
) in
[0,T]\times I
obtained in Theorems
4.2
.
Moreover, throughout this section, we always assume that (
5.1
) holds.
Since the
H^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
-estimates are the same as those presented in Lemmas
7.2
–
7.7
, it suffices to focus on the
D^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
-estimates and the time-weighted
D^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
-estimates.

8.1.
The third-order estimates of the velocity

First, following the proofs of Lemmas
7.8
–
7.9
, we can derive the following estimates:

Lemma 8.1
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that, for any
t\in[0,T]
,

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)(t)\Big|_{2}+\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{rrr},\frac{u_{rr}}{r},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{rr},\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)(t)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle+\Big|\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)(t)\Big|_{\infty}+\int_{0}^{t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|^{2}_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s+\int_{0}^{t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{trr},\big(\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T).

Proof.

We divide the proof into two steps.

1.
First, it follows from (
7.59
) that, for all
t\in[\tau,T]
and
\tau\in(0,T)
,

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+1\Big).

Integrating the above over
[\tau,t]
, together with Lemma
7.1
, yields

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)(t)\Big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s

(8.1)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)(\tau)\Big|_{2}^{2}+C(T)\Big(1+\int_{\tau}^{t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)(\tau)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big).

For the
L^{2}(I)
-boundedness of
r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r})(\tau)
on the right-hand side
of the above, we apply
r^{\frac{m}{2}}\partial_{r}
to
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
and multiply
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}

by
r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}
, respectively, and then take the
L^{2}(I)
-norm of these two resulting equality to obtain

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)(\tau)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|(u,\psi)(\tau)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{rr}(\tau)|_{2}

(8.2)

\displaystyle+C_{0}\Big|\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)(\tau)\Big|_{\infty}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{r},\psi_{r},\frac{\psi}{r}\Big)(\tau)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle+\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\phi_{rr},\frac{\phi_{r}}{r}\big)(\tau)\Big|_{2}+\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{rr},\frac{1}{r}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big)(\tau)\Big|_{2},

which, along with the time continuity of
(\phi,u,\psi)
, Lemmas
7.1
,
A.1
,
and
B.1
, yields

\displaystyle\limsup_{\tau\to 0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)(\tau)\Big|_{2}

(8.3)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|(u_{0},\psi_{0})|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{0})_{rr}|_{2}+C_{0}\Big|\big((u_{0})_{r},\frac{u_{0}}{r}\big)\Big|_{\infty}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big((u_{0})_{r},(\psi_{0})_{r},\frac{\psi_{0}}{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle+\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big((\phi_{0})_{rr},\frac{(\phi_{0})_{r}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}+\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\big((u_{0})_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u_{0}\big)_{rr},\frac{1}{r}\big((u_{0})_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u_{0}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\|(\boldsymbol{u}_{0},\boldsymbol{\psi}_{0},\nabla\boldsymbol{u}_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|(\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u}_{0},\nabla\boldsymbol{u}_{0},\nabla\boldsymbol{\psi}_{0})\|_{L^{2}}+C_{0}\|(\nabla^{2}\phi_{0},\nabla^{3}\boldsymbol{u}_{0})\|_{L^{2}}\leq C_{0}.

Consequently, based on the above discussions, letting
\tau\to 0
in (
8.1
), we obtain
from the Grönwall inequality that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)(t)\Big|_{2}+\int_{0}^{t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T).

(8.4)

2.
Next, based on the estimates in (
7.44
) and (
7.54
)–(
7.55
), following the calculations of (
7.63
)–(
7.65
) in Lemma
7.9
without the time-weight
\sqrt{t}
,
we have

\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{rrr},\frac{u_{rr}}{r},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{rr},\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)(t)\Big|_{2}\!+\!\Big|\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)(t)\Big|_{\infty}\!+\!\int_{0}^{t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{trr},\big(\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\!\leq\!C(T).

This completes the proof of Lemma
8.1
.
∎

Next, we derive the higher-order estimates for
\psi
:

Lemma 8.2
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that, for any
t\in[0,T]
,

\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\psi_{rr},\big(\frac{\psi}{r}\big)_{r},\psi_{tr},\frac{\psi_{t}}{r}\Big)(t)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T).

Proof.

We divide the proof into two steps.

1.

Applying
\partial_{l}
(
l=1,\cdots\!,n)
to both sides of (
7.46
) leads to the following equation in the sense of distributions:

(\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v})_{t}+\diver\big(\boldsymbol{u}(\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v})\big)=\tilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\prime},

(8.5)

where

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\prime}

\displaystyle:=(\diver\boldsymbol{u})(\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v})-\partial_{l}\boldsymbol{u}\cdot\nabla(\diver\boldsymbol{v})-\partial_{l}(\nabla\boldsymbol{u}^{\top}:\nabla\boldsymbol{v})

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma-1}{2\alpha}\nabla(\partial_{l}\phi)\cdot(\boldsymbol{v}-\boldsymbol{u})-\frac{\gamma-1}{2\alpha}\nabla\phi\cdot(\partial_{l}\boldsymbol{v}-\partial_{l}\boldsymbol{u})

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma-1}{2\alpha}\partial_{l}\phi(\diver\boldsymbol{v}-\diver\boldsymbol{u})-\frac{\gamma-1}{2\alpha}\phi(\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v}-\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{u}).

It can be checked due to
\boldsymbol{v}=\boldsymbol{u}+2\alpha\nabla\log\rho
, and Lemmas
3.4
and
A.1
that

\nabla\diver\boldsymbol{v}\in L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{n})),\quad\boldsymbol{u}\in L^{1}([0,T];W^{1,\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{n})),

(8.6)

and
\tilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\prime}\in L^{1}([0,T];L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{n}))
, since

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\mathcal{G}}^{\prime}\|_{L^{1}([0,T];L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{n}))}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{1}([0,T];L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{n}))}\|\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{v}\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{n}))}

(8.7)

\displaystyle+C_{0}\|\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{1}([0,T];L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{n}))}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{n}))}

\displaystyle+C_{0}\|\nabla^{2}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{n}))}\|(\boldsymbol{u},\boldsymbol{v})\|_{L^{1}([0,T];L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{n}))}

\displaystyle+C_{0}\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{n})}\|(\nabla\boldsymbol{u},\nabla\boldsymbol{v})\|_{L^{1}([0,T];L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{n}))}

\displaystyle+C_{0}\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{n})}\|(\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u},\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{v})\|_{L^{1}([0,T];L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{n}))}<\infty.

Then it follows from Lemma
A.12
that, for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
,

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\gamma-1}{\alpha}\big\|\phi^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

(8.8)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Big((\diver\boldsymbol{u})(\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v})^{2}-2\big(\partial_{l}\boldsymbol{u}\cdot\nabla\diver\boldsymbol{v}\big)(\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v})\Big)\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}

\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\partial_{l}(\nabla\boldsymbol{u}^{\top}:\nabla\boldsymbol{v})(\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v})\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}-\frac{\gamma-1}{\alpha}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\big(\nabla(\partial_{l}\phi)\cdot(\boldsymbol{v}-\boldsymbol{u})\big)(\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v})\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma-1}{\alpha}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Big(\nabla\phi\cdot(\partial_{l}\boldsymbol{v}-\partial_{l}\boldsymbol{u})+\partial_{l}\phi(\diver\boldsymbol{v}-\diver\boldsymbol{u})\Big)(\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v})\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}

\displaystyle+\frac{\gamma-1}{\alpha}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\phi(\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{u})(\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v})\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}.

Summing above over
l=1,\cdots\!,n
, together with the spherical coordinate transformation, yields that, for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
,

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\gamma-1}{\alpha}\Big|(r^{m}\phi)^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}

(8.9)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\big(\frac{m}{r}u-u_{r}\big)\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r-2\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\Big(u_{r}v_{r}+\frac{m}{r^{2}}uv\Big)_{r}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma-1}{\alpha}\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi_{rr}(v-u)\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma-1}{\alpha}\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi_{r}\Big(\big(2v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)-\big(2u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big)\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+\frac{\gamma-1}{\alpha}\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r:=\sum_{i=16}^{20}\mathcal{G}_{i}.

For
\mathcal{G}_{16}
–
\mathcal{G}_{20}
, it follows from (
7.50
), Lemmas
4.7
,
5.3
,
7.1
,
7.5
,
8.1
,
A.1
, and
A.3
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{16}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{\infty}\Big|\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)\Big|\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2},

(8.10)

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{17}

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\Big(u_{rr}v_{r}+u_{r}v_{rr}+m\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\frac{v}{r}+m\frac{u}{r}\big(\frac{v}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}u_{rr}|_{2}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}rv_{r}|_{\infty}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{rr}|_{2}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}v_{r}|_{\infty}\big)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|u_{r}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}v_{rr}|_{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}\Big(|v|_{\infty}\Big|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}+\Big|\frac{u}{r}\Big|_{\infty}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\frac{v}{r}\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}\Big)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{3}{2}}(v_{r},v_{rr})|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}(v_{r},v_{rr})|_{2}\Big)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C(T)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{3-m}{2}}|^{2}_{\infty}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-\frac{m}{2}}|^{2}_{\infty}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(v_{r},v_{rr})|^{2}_{2}+\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|^{2}_{2}+1\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}+1\Big),

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{18}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|(u,v)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{rr}|_{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}+1\Big),

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{19}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}\big|(\chi_{1}^{\flat}rv_{r},v)\big|_{\infty}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}v_{r}|_{\infty}\big)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}\Big|\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{\infty}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{3}{2}}(v_{r},v_{rr})|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}(v_{r},v_{rr})|_{2}+1\big)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{3-m}{2}}|_{\infty}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-\frac{m}{2}}|_{\infty}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(v_{r},v_{rr})|_{2}+1\Big)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}+1\Big),

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{20}

\displaystyle\leq C|\phi|_{\infty}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}+1\Big).

Thus, combining with (
8.9
)–(
8.10
) leads to

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}+1\Big),

(8.11)

which, along with Lemmas
7.1
and
B.1
, and the Grönwall inequality, yields that

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(v_{rr},\big(\frac{v}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)(t)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\|\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+1\big)

(8.12)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\|(\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u}_{0},\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{\psi}_{0})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+1\big)\leq C(T).

Consequently, it follows from the above estimate, (
7.3
), and Lemma
7.5
that

\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\psi_{rr},\big(\frac{\psi}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)(t)\Big|_{2}\leq C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(v_{rr},\big(\frac{v}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)(t)\Big|_{2}+C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{rr},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)(t)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T).

(8.13)

2.
Finally, multiplying
\eqref{e2.2}_{3}
by
r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}
and applying
r^{\frac{m}{2}}\partial_{r}
to
\eqref{e2.2}_{3}
, respectively, then taking the
L^{2}(I)
-norm of these two resulting equality, we obtain from (
8.13
) and Lemmas
7.5
–
7.6
and
8.1
that

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\psi_{tr},\frac{\psi_{t}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big((\psi u)_{rr},\frac{1}{r}(\psi u)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}+\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{rr},\frac{1}{r}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}

(8.14)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\psi_{rr}u,\psi_{r}u_{r},\psi u_{rr},\psi_{r}\frac{u}{r},\frac{\psi}{r}u_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{rrr},\frac{u_{rr}}{r},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{rr},\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|(u,\psi)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{rr},\psi_{rr})|_{2}+C_{0}\Big|\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{\infty}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\psi_{r},\frac{\psi}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{rrr},\frac{u_{rr}}{r},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{rr},\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T).

The proof of Lemma
8.2
is completed.
∎

We now derive the higher-order estimates for
(\phi,u)
.

Lemma 8.3
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\phi_{rrr},\big(\frac{\phi_{r}}{r}\big)_{r},\phi_{trr},\frac{\phi_{tr}}{r}\Big)(t)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{rrrr},\big(\frac{u_{rr}}{r}\big)_{r},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{rrr},\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\mbox{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

We divide the proof into two steps.

1.
First, for the
L^{2}(I)
-estimate of
r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\phi_{rrr},(\frac{\phi_{r}}{r})_{r}\big)
, it follows from Lemmas
4.7
,
7.6
–
7.7
, and
8.2
that

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\frac{\phi_{r}}{r}\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\phi\frac{\psi}{r}\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}\leq C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\frac{\psi}{r}\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}+C_{0}|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}\leq C(T),

(8.15)

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{rrr}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{rr}\psi|_{2}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}\psi_{r}|_{2}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi\psi_{rr}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{rr}\psi|_{2}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi\psi\psi_{r}|_{2}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi\psi_{rr}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|(\phi,\psi)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{rr},\psi_{rr})|_{2}+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{r}|_{2}\leq C(T).

Notice from Lemmas
4.7
,
7.6
–
7.7
, and
8.2
that

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\phi_{trr},\frac{\phi_{tr}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big((\phi\psi)_{tr},\frac{1}{r}(\phi\psi)_{t}\Big)\Big|_{2}

(8.16)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|(\phi,\psi)|_{\infty}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\phi_{tr},\psi_{tr},\frac{\psi_{t}}{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\phi_{t}\psi|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}\psi_{t}|_{2}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{t}\psi_{r}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}\psi_{t}|_{2}+C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{t}\big(\psi_{r},\frac{\psi}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}.

For the estimates of
|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}\psi_{t}|_{2}
and
|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{t}(\psi_{r},\frac{\psi}{r})|_{2}

on the right-hand side of the above, it follows from Lemmas
7.5
–
7.7
,
8.2
,
A.1
, and
A.3
that

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}\psi_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}\psi_{t}|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}\psi_{t}|_{2}

(8.17)

\displaystyle\leq|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r\phi_{r}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\psi_{t}|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}\phi_{r}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{3}{2}}(\phi_{r},\phi_{rr})|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}(\phi_{r},\phi_{rr})|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{3-m}{2}}|_{\infty}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-\frac{m}{2}}|_{\infty}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{r},\phi_{rr})|_{2}\leq C(T),

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{t}\big(\psi_{r},\frac{\psi}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Big|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{t}\big(\psi_{r},\frac{\psi}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}+\Big|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{t}\big(\psi_{r},\frac{\psi}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}

(8.18)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{m+2}{2}}\phi_{t}\big(\psi_{r},\frac{\psi}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}+C_{0}\Big|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{m+2}{2}}\phi_{tr}\big(\psi_{r},\frac{\psi}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}\Big|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{m+2}{2}}\phi_{t}\Big(\psi_{rr},\big(\frac{\psi}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}\phi_{t}|_{\infty}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\psi_{r},\frac{\psi}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|(\chi_{1}^{\flat}r\psi_{r},\psi)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{t},\phi_{tr})|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r\phi_{t}|_{\infty}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\psi_{rr},\big(\frac{\psi}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}+C(T)|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}\phi_{t}|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{3}{2}}(\psi_{r},\psi_{rr},\phi_{t},\phi_{tr})|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}(\phi_{t},\phi_{tr})|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\psi_{r},\psi_{rr},\phi_{t},\phi_{tr})|_{2}\big)\leq C(T).

Combining with (
8.16
)–(
8.18
) yields that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\phi_{trr},\frac{\phi_{tr}}{r}\big)(t)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T).

(8.19)

2.
First, multiplying
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
by
\frac{1}{r}
, applying
r^{\frac{m}{2}}\partial_{r}
, and taking the
L^{2}(I)
-norm of both sides of the resulting equality, we obtain from (
8.15
), and Lemmas
7.5
–
7.6
and
8.1
that

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big)_{r}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\big(\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)_{r},\big(\frac{u}{r}u_{r}\big)_{r},\big(\frac{\phi_{r}}{r}\big)_{r},2\alpha\big(\frac{\psi}{r}u_{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}

(8.20)

\displaystyle\leq\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\big(\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)_{r},\big(\frac{\phi_{r}}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}+C_{0}|(u,\psi)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}u_{rr}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r},\big(\frac{\psi}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}|u_{r}|_{\infty}\leq\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}+C(T).

Taking the square of above and then integrating over
[0,t]
, together with Lemma
8.1
, yield that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

\int_{0}^{t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T).

(8.21)

Next, applying
r^{\frac{m}{2}}\partial_{r}^{2}
to
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
and taking
L^{2}(I)
-norm of the resulting equality, we first obtain
from (
8.15
), and Lemmas
7.5
–
7.6
and
8.1
–
8.2
that

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{rrr}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{trr},(uu_{r})_{rr},\phi_{rrr},2\alpha(\psi u_{r})_{rr}\big)\big|_{2}

(8.22)

\displaystyle\leq|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{trr},\phi_{rrr})|_{2}+|(u,\psi)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{rrr}|_{2}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{rr}\psi_{r}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{rr},\psi_{rr})|_{2}|u_{r}|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{trr}|_{2}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{rr}\psi_{r}|_{2}+C(T).

Then, for the estimate of
|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{rr}\psi_{r}|_{2}
on the right-hand side of the above,
it follows from Lemmas
7.5
–
7.6
,
8.1
–
8.2
,
A.1
,
and
A.3
that

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{rr}\psi_{r}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{rr}\psi_{r}|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{rr}\psi_{r}|_{2}

(8.23)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{m+2}{2}}(u_{rr}\psi_{r},u_{rrr}\psi_{r},u_{rr}\psi_{rr})|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{rr}\psi_{r}|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{rr},u_{rrr})|_{2}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r\psi_{r}|_{\infty}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{rr}|_{2}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}ru_{rr}|_{\infty}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}\psi_{r}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{rr}|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{3}{2}}(\psi_{r},\psi_{rr},u_{rr},u_{rrr})|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}(\psi_{r},\psi_{rr})|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{3-m}{2}}|_{\infty}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-\frac{m}{2}}|_{\infty}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{rr},u_{rrr},\psi_{r},\psi_{rr})|_{2}\leq C(T).

Thus, combining (
8.22
)–(
8.23
) gives

\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{rrr}\Big|_{2}\leq|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{trr}|_{2}+C(T).

(8.24)

Taking the square of the above and then integrating over
[0,t]
, together with Lemma
8.1
, give that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

\int_{0}^{t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{rrr}\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T),

(8.25)

which, along with (
8.21
), and Lemma
7.1
, leads to

\int_{0}^{t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{rrrr},\big(\frac{u_{rr}}{r}\big)_{r},\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{rrr},\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{u}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T).

(8.26)

This completes the proof.
∎

8.2.
Time-weighted estimates of the velocity

We now establish the time-weighted fourth-order tangential estimates for
u
.

Lemma 8.4
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

\begin{split}&t|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}(t)|^{2}_{2}+\int_{0}^{t}s\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{ttr},\frac{u_{tt}}{r}\big)\Big|^{2}_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\mbox{for any $t\in[0,T]$}.\end{split}

Proof.

We divide the proof into three steps.

1.
We give some estimates to be used later. First, it follows from Lemmas
7.4
,
8.1
,
A.1
, and
A.3
that

\displaystyle|u_{t}|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|(u_{t},u_{tr})|_{2}\leq C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}(u_{t},u_{tr})|_{2}+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}(u_{t},u_{tr})|_{2}

(8.27)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r(u_{t},u_{tr},u_{trr})|_{2}+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}(u_{t},u_{tr})|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{t},u_{tr},u_{trr})|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{trr}|_{2}+1\big),

\displaystyle|\chi_{1}^{\flat}ru_{tr}|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{3}{2}}(u_{tr},u_{trr})|_{2}\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{tr},u_{trr})|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{trr}|_{2}+1\big),

\displaystyle|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}u_{tr}|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}(u_{tr},u_{trr})|_{2}\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{tr},u_{trr})|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{trr}|_{2}+1\big).

Next, applying
r^{\frac{m}{2}}\partial_{t}
to
\eqref{e2.2}_{3}
and taking the
L^{2}(I)
-norm of both sides of the resulting equality, we obtain from (
8.27
), and
Lemmas
7.5
–
7.6
and
8.1
–
8.2
that

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big((\psi u)_{tr},\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{tr}\Big)\Big|_{2}

(8.28)

\displaystyle\leq|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tr}|_{2}+|u_{r}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{t}|_{2}+|u_{t}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{r}|_{2}+|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{tr}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{trr},\big(\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{trr},\big(\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}+1\Big).

Finally, based on
\eqref{e2.2}_{1}
, it follows from Lemmas
4.7
,
7.4
–
7.7
,
and
8.1
that

\displaystyle|\phi_{t}|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|\Big(u\phi_{r},\phi\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big)\Big|_{\infty}\leq C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}\Big(|u|_{\infty}|\psi|_{\infty}+\Big|\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{\infty}\Big)\leq C(T),

(8.29)

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u\phi_{r})_{t}|_{2}+C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(\phi\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big)_{t}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{tr}|_{2}+|\phi|_{\infty}|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{t}|_{2}\Big|\big(u_{r},\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big|_{\infty}+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tr},\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T),

which, along with the chain rule, (
8.28
), and Lemma
7.5
, leads to

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{ttr}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{tt}\psi,\phi_{t}\psi_{t},\phi\psi_{tt})\big|_{2}\leq C_{0}\big(|(\phi,\psi)|_{\infty}\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{tt},\psi_{tt})\big|_{2}+|\phi_{t}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{t}|_{2}\big)

(8.30)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{trr},\big(\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}+1\Big).

2.
We now proceed to prove Lemma
8.4
.
Formally, applying
r^{m}u_{tt}\partial_{t}^{2}
to both sides of
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
and integrating the resulting equality over
I
yield that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}+2\alpha\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{ttr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{tt}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

(8.31)

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}(uu_{r})_{tt}u_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}r-\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi_{ttr}u_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}r+2\alpha\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}(\psi u_{r})_{tt}u_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}r:=\sum_{i=21}^{23}\mathcal{G}_{i}

for
a.e.

t\in(\tau,T)
and
\tau\in(0,T)
. Here, we temporarily assume that the above energy equality holds, and the specific proof will be provided in Step 3 below.

Then, for
\mathcal{G}_{21}
–
\mathcal{G}_{22}
, it follows from (
8.27
), (
8.30
), Lemmas
7.1
,
7.5
, and
8.1
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{21}

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}(u_{tt}u_{r}+2u_{t}u_{tr}+uu_{ttr})u_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}r

(8.32)

\displaystyle\leq|u_{r}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}+2|u_{t}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tr}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}+|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{ttr}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}+1\big)+\frac{\alpha}{8}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{ttr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{tt}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{22}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{trr},\big(\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+1\Big)+\frac{\alpha}{8}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{ttr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{tt}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}.

For
\mathcal{G}_{23}
, it follows from
\eqref{e2.2}_{3}
, (
8.27
)–(
8.28
),
Lemmas
7.1
,
7.5
–
7.6
, and
8.1
–
8.2
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{23}

\displaystyle=-2\alpha\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}(\psi_{tt}u_{r}+2\psi_{t}u_{tr}+\psi u_{ttr})u_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}r

(8.33)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|u_{r}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{tt}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}ru_{tr}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\psi_{t}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}u_{tr}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{t}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}+C_{0}|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{ttr}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{trr},\big(\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{ttr}|_{2}+1\Big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}+C(T)\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{trr},\big(\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}+1\Big)+\frac{\alpha}{8}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{ttr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{tt}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}.

Thus, collecting (
8.31
)–(
8.33
), along with Lemma
7.1
, gives

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}+\alpha\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{ttr},\frac{u_{tt}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}+\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\Big(u_{trr},\big(\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+1\Big).

Multiplying the above by
t
and integrating the resulting inequality over
[\tau,t]
for
\tau\in(0,t)
, along with Lemmas
8.1
and
8.3
, imply that

t|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{t}s\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{ttr},\frac{u_{tt}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\big(\tau|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}(\tau)|_{2}^{2}+1\big).

(8.34)

Next, thanks to Lemma
8.1
,
r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}\in L^{2}([0,T];L^{2}(I))
, it follows from Lemma
A.6
that there exists a sequence
\{\tau_{k}\}_{k=1}^{\infty}
such that

\tau_{k}\to 0,\quad\,\tau_{k}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}(\tau_{k})|_{2}^{2}\to 0\qquad\,\,\text{as $k\to\infty$}.

(8.35)

Choosing
\tau=\tau_{k}\to 0
in (
8.34
) yields that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

t|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}s\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{ttr},\frac{u_{tt}}{r}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T).

(8.36)

3.
In the final step, we give a rigorous proof of the energy equality (
8.31
). Suppose that
\boldsymbol{\varphi}(\boldsymbol{x})=\varphi(r)\frac{\boldsymbol{x}}{r}
is any given spherically symmetric vector function satisfying
r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\varphi,\varphi_{r},\frac{\varphi}{r})\in L^{2}(I)
. Of course, by Lemma
B.1
, this is equivalent to
\boldsymbol{\varphi}\in H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
.
Then applying
r^{m}\varphi\partial_{t}
to both sides of
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
and integrating the resulting equality over
I
yield from integration by parts that
